The scarcity of large-scale labeled data motivates adapting pretrained vision models rather than training task-specific architectures from scratch, raising a fundamental question: do general-purpose vision foundation models encode PAD-relevant information accessible with minimal task-specific training? To investigate, we systematically evaluate 24 frozen encoders, including self-supervised vision transformers, vision-language encoders, and supervised CNNs, using a unified linear-probing protocol on the MCIO benchmark (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, OULU-NPU). The backbone remains fixed, and only a lightweight linear head is trained to isolate the PAD information already present in the pretrained representation. %We report intra- and cross-dataset performance, along with accuracy-compute trade-offs, relative to two specialist PAD baselines. Results show that frozen foundation-model representations can support strong intra-dataset PAD performance with only a linear classifier, but this performance does not reliably transfer across datasets. Model scale is beneficial within several families, although the effect is not monotonic and is strongly mediated by architecture and pretraining. InternViT-6B achieves the lowest mean intra-dataset error, whereas CLIP ViT-B/32 offers the most favorable cross-dataset transfer-compute trade-off among the evaluated probes. These findings suggest that while pretrained representations contain PAD-relevant information, explicit adaptation remains necessary to address domain shift.

</details>


### [72] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可控干预实验和证书门控协议，揭示了潜在世界模型中物理参数的可识别性由输入和预测目标共同决定：输入限制可知内容，预测目标决定保留内容；仅当预测目标与物理参数直接相关时（如触摸预测保留刚度），该参数才会进入潜在表征，且数据量仅能改善已获得的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究潜在世界模型中，哪些物理量会被模型学习到，以及决定因素是什么，旨在区分环境限制与目标函数限制对潜在表征的影响。

Method: 在POKEWORLD环境中进行可控干预实验，使用证书门控协议先验证参数是否可从原始观测恢复，再测量其是否进入潜在表征；在RH20T真实数据上通过输入-目标因子设计验证结果的可迁移性。

Result: 输入限制可知内容，预测目标决定保留内容；刚度仅当预测触摸时进入潜在表征（R²=0.50 vs -0.02）；单步预测下视觉潜在表征会丢失完全可见的对象状态；拖拽参数在所有确定性预测目标下均无法有效获取（可恢复性0.89，预测目标下0.13，监督头0.45）；RH20T上验证了两机制的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 目标函数的结构决定了潜在表征获取哪些物理参数，增加数据量仅能改善已获取的参数，而非扩大可获取参数的范围。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [73] [TreeCCA: Canonical Correlation Analysis via Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27027)
*James Chapman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TreeCCA，首个将梯度提升树作为CCA编码器的方法，通过Eckart-Young损失实现端到端训练，兼具非线性精度与原生可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型相关分析（CCA）长期以来依赖线性或神经网络编码器，而梯度提升树在表格数据中占主导地位，但尚未被用于CCA编码。现有方法缺乏即插即用的可靠性与原生可解释性。

Method: 提出Eckart-Young (EY)损失函数，为每个样本提供闭式梯度，可直接作为自定义目标函数嵌入标准GBT库（XGBoost、LightGBM）。TreeCCA利用树模型的分裂特性天然提供特征重要性解释。

Result: 在合成基准上匹配或超越Deep CCA（Signed Power: 2.61 vs 2.43; Hermite: 2.93 vs 2.89）；在稀疏基准上PMD无信号时TreeCCA Precision@S=1.00；UCI HAR上精度相当但成本降低5倍，且增益重要性验证了物理假设。

Conclusion: TreeCCA是首个结合非线性精度与原生可解释性的CCA方法，在多个表格多视图数据集上匹配或超越线性CCA，为无架构设计的即插即用CCA提供了新范式。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees dominate tabular machine learning, yet canonical correlation analysis has always relied on linear or neural encoders. We propose \textbf{TreeCCA}, the first method to train gradient-boosted tree ensembles end-to-end as CCA encoders, inheriting their plug-and-play reliability: no architecture design, familiar hyperparameters, and strong performance with defaults. The technical enabler is the Eckart-Young (EY) loss, which supplies closed-form per-sample gradients that slot directly into any standard GBT library (XGBoost, LightGBM) as a custom objective.
  TreeCCA is the first CCA method to combine nonlinear accuracy with native interpretability: every tree split selects one feature, so gain importances reveal which inputs drive cross-view correlation at no extra cost. We demonstrate these properties on synthetic benchmarks, where TreeCCA matches or exceeds Deep CCA (2.61 vs.\ 2.43 on Signed Power; 2.93 vs.\ 2.89 on Hermite), and on a sparse benchmark with zero linear cross-view covariance, where TreeCCA recovers the true support with $\text{Precision@}S = 1.00$ at $p=50$ while PMD finds no signal. On the UCI HAR sensor-fusion benchmark, TreeCCA achieves comparable accuracy to Deep CCA at $5\times$ lower cost, while XGBoost gain importances directly validate a physics-motivated hypothesis about the data --- an interpretation not readily available with neural encoders. Across five popular tabular multi-view datasets, TreeMCCA consistently matches or exceeds linear CCA in both nonlinear correlation extraction and downstream classification accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [Lottery Tickets Are Not Deployment Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27031)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据（LT）模型在实际部署条件下能否无缝替换密集模型的问题。尽管稀疏候选模型在干净准确率上与密集模型匹配，但在校准、OOD响应、类别级可靠性、表征和下游决策行为上存在显著差异。彩票票据替换在固定阈值策略下会导致7%-10%的决策变更（接受/审查），违背了"即插即用"的初衷。理论证明，即使逐点top-1预测完全一致，也无法保证固定阈值决策不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献关于稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据如何改变模型行为的结论相互矛盾，且未考虑实际部署场景中决策逻辑已固定的情况。作者希望从实际部署角度评估这些混合发现，研究精度匹配的彩票票据或其他稀疏模型能否在不重新配置下游决策逻辑的前提下替换密集模型。

Method: 作者在部署层面研究生产替换问题，审计了涵盖校准、OOD响应、类别级可靠性、表征和下游策略决策的广泛协议特定行为面板，通过行为兼容性距离总结排除干净准确率后的偏差。进行了大量实验，并构建了理论分析来解释路由结果。

Result: 稀疏候选模型能恢复密集参考模型的准确率，但行为上仍然存在差异；在多个研究带匹配设置中，LT还表现出更低的损坏准确率。在小差距设置中，彩票票据替换会导致7%-10%的接受/审查决策变更，这恰恰产生了"即插即用"本应避免的负担。

Conclusion: 干净准确率认证存在局限性：与固定密集模型的兼容性评估与将变更归因于稀疏性或将每个测量偏差视为有害是不同的问题。理论证明，即使精确的逐点top-1一致也无法约束固定阈值决策变化，操作边界附近的小置信度偏移会产生一阶路由变更。

Abstract: Reports on how sparsification, compression, and lottery tickets change model behavior have been mixed in the prior literature, with beneficial effects observed in some studies and adverse effects in others. Moreover, prior work has not considered actual deployment conditions, where decision logic is already fixed for the incumbent. To assess these mixed findings from a practical standpoint, we study the production-replacement question at the deployment level, namely whether an accuracy-matched lottery ticket or another sparse challenger can replace an incumbent dense model without reconfiguring downstream decision logic. We therefore audit a broad, protocol-specific panel of deployment-relevant behaviors spanning calibration, OOD response, class-level reliability, representations, and downstream policy decisions, and summarize clean-accuracy-excluded deviations with a behavioral-compatibility distance. Across extensive experiments, sparse candidates repeatedly recover dense-reference accuracy yet remain behaviorally different; in several study-band-matched settings, LTs also show lower corruption accuracy. In small-gap settings with fixed-threshold policy diagnostics, lottery-ticket replacement changes 7% to 10% of accept--review decisions. This churn creates precisely the burden that drop-in replacement is meant to avoid: reconfiguring and revalidating downstream decision logic. These findings establish the limits of clean-accuracy certification: Establishing compatibility with a fixed incumbent is distinct from attributing churn uniquely to sparsity or treating every measured deviation as harmful. Our theory explains the routing result: Even exact pointwise top-1 agreement cannot bound fixed-threshold decision changes, and small confidence shifts near the operating boundary can generate first-order routing churn.

</details>


### [75] [Single-Beat Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation Using Ear-PPG and ECG with a Lightweight Hybrid Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27076)
*Kindeep K. Dhatt,Tengyue Wu,Hanbang Hua,Yayun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级多模态可穿戴框架，通过整合单拍心电、耳夹反射式光电容积描记术（PPG）与惯性测量单元（IMU）数据，使用混合学习架构（1D-CNN + LightGBM）实现无需长时间窗口的逐拍连续无袖带血压估计。在压力协议和PulseDB数据集上，系统对收缩压和舒张压的平均绝对误差分别为4.02 mmHg和1.79 mmHg，相比基线模型综合MAE降低28.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无袖带连续血压监测方法在动态条件下受到运动伪影、生理变异性以及传统脉搏波传导时间（PTT）模型鲁棒性不足的限制。许多方法依赖多秒时间窗口来稳定估计，但这一假设在真实世界监测中常因间歇性信号损坏而失效。因此，需要一种能够在逐拍水平进行可靠估计且适合可穿戴设备部署的轻量级框架。

Method: 系统集成了同步胸部心电（ECG）和耳夹反射式PPG，每个传感器均与6轴惯性测量单元（IMU）共位以提供运动上下文。提出混合学习架构：1D-CNN从单个PPG拍中提取64维嵌入，并与30个基于生理学的特征（包括PTT统计和心率变异性）融合，随后通过LightGBM回归进行血压估计。采用多阶段压力协议（n=10）和PulseDB公开数据集进行主体分离验证。

Result: 在30次独立实验中，模型对收缩压的平均绝对误差为4.02 ± 0.21 mmHg，对舒张压为1.79 ± 0.05 mmHg，综合MAE相比基线模型降低28.2%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无需长时间上下文的逐拍血压估计，支持在资源受限条件下进行可穿戴部署的计算高效型无袖带血压监测。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring remains challenging due to motion artifacts, physiological variability, and the limited robustness of conventional pulse transit time (PTT) models under dynamic conditions. Many prior approaches rely on multi-second windows to stabilize estimation, an assumption that is frequently violated during real-world monitoring with intermittent signal corruption. Here, we show that discriminative BP-related information is preserved at the single-beat level and present a lightweight multi-modal wearable framework for continuous BP estimation. The system integrates synchronized chest electrocardiography (ECG) and ear-clip reflectance photoplethysmography, each co-located with a 6-axis inertial measurement unit to provide motion context. We introduce a hybrid learning architecture in which a one-dimensional convolutional neural network extracts a 64-dimensional embedding from individual PPG beats and fuses it with 30 physiology-grounded features, including PTT statistics and heart rate variability, followed by LightGBM regression. The method was evaluated using a multi-phase stress protocol ($n=10$) and the PulseDB public dataset with subject-disjoint validation. Across 30 independent runs, the model achieved mean absolute errors of $4.02 \pm 0.21$~mmHg for systolic BP and $1.79 \pm 0.05$~mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to a 28.2\% reduction in combined MAE relative to baseline models. By enabling beat-wise estimation without long temporal context, this framework supports computationally efficient cuffless BP monitoring suitable for wearable deployment under practical resource constraints. The source code for this work is available at https://github.com/SYMBIOX-Lab/BP-wireless.

</details>


### [76] [Equilibrium Training of Energy-Based Models with Parallel Trajectory Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27077)
*Nicolas Béreux,Aurélien Decelle,Cyril Furtlehner,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于平行轨迹退火（PTT）的训练算法，通过利用优化路径的连续性在训练过程中维持平衡采样，解决了能量基模型（EBM）中马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛混合不佳的问题。PTT在计算成本与持久对比散度相当的前提下，提供热化时间估计、平衡样本和准确对数似然，在受限玻尔兹曼机和离散表格数据上均优于现有EBM训练方法和深度生成模型，实现了实用且高效的EBM平衡最大似然训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量基模型（EBM）虽为科学数据生成建模提供可解释框架，但马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛混合性能差，导致训练不稳定且可靠性受限，亟需一种能维持平衡采样的高效训练算法。

Method: 提出平行轨迹退火（PTT）训练算法，利用优化路径连续性维持训练过程中的平衡采样，结合储层采样和自适应优化，计算成本与持久对比散度相当。

Result: 在受限玻尔兹曼机上PTT持续优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上超越深度生成模型，生成更高质量样本，对过拟合和有限数据具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PTT使EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用且计算高效，为科学数据和离散表格数据生成建模提供了可靠的训练范式。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [77] [Sky sphere representation in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27092)
*Aleksandr Berdnikov,Yevgeny Liokumovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现参数量约100B的语言模型在残差流中编码了可解码的夜空星图表示，该表示在多个开源模型中存在，且是一种弯曲的高维不可约特征流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（~100B参数）内部是否编码了关于物理世界（夜空星图）的结构化几何表示，而非仅存储事实性知识。

Method: 从语言模型残差流中解码夜空星图表示；通过主成分分析（PCA）检验表示是否出现在顶层主成分中；采用留一法（LOO）测试评估表示的鲁棒性；验证该表示并非来自相关平面表示的简单泄露。

Result: 大多数开源模型在残差流中存在夜空星图表示；表示出现在与夜空相关提示的顶层主成分中；LOO测试中R²分数达65-85%，中位角度误差为12°-21°；该表示是弯曲的高维不可约特征流形。

Conclusion: 大语言模型内部编码了夜空的弯曲几何表示，这是首次发现高维弯曲不可约特征流形，表明模型可能以类似人类认知地图的方式组织空间知识。

Abstract: We analyze whether language models of size ~100B have a representation of the night sky map that is decodable from their residual stream. We find that most of the considered open-source models do have such a representation, and it often even surfaces to the top principal components on prompts that ask questions like ``what is close to this object in the night sky''. In all but one model this representation showed significant scores in LOO testing, containing up to 65-85% of variance ($R^2$-score) and having median angular error down to $12^\circ-21^\circ$. We verify that our representation is not a simple leak from a correlated flat representation. To our knowledge, this representation is the first example of a curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold.
  Codes used in the paper are published at https://github.com/l3erdnik/Decodable-sky

</details>


### [78] [Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Coherent Emergency Department Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27106)
*Filipa Lino,Bárbara Tavares,Carlos Santiago,Cláudia Soares,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HierSTT，一种层次化Transformer框架，用于同时预测医院、区域和国家三级急诊需求，通过端到端联合建模和一致性损失函数，在葡萄牙81家医院数据集上相比非层次化基线降低32%的WAPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ED需求预测方法通常仅在单一层级独立进行，忽略了医院-区域-国家之间的层级结构，导致预测结果在跨层级聚合时存在不一致性，无法满足不同决策层级（医院排班、区域协调、国家规划）的协调需求。

Method: HierSTT包括：(1) Temporal Fusion Transformer捕捉国家级动态；(2) 时空Transformer编码器-解码器模块，在高层级预测条件约束下建模区域和医院需求；(3) 一致性感知损失函数，在训练中惩罚跨层级的不一致性。

Result: 在覆盖81家医院、5个区域卫生管理局的葡萄牙全国ED数据集上，HierSTT相比最佳非层次化深度学习基线降低32%的WAPE，并优于所有经典层次化调和(reconciliation)方法，同时实现近乎一致的跨层级预测。

Conclusion: 层次化端到端建模能有效解决ED需求预测中的跨层级不一致问题，为多级医疗决策提供更可靠的预测支持。

Abstract: Emergency Departments (EDs) are critical access points in healthcare systems, yet they face persistent pressure from unpredictable patient demand, seasonal surges, and non-urgent visits. Effective ED planning requires forecasts at multiple decision-making levels: hospitals need local demand estimates for staffing and bed management, regions require forecasts to coordinate healthcare units, and national authorities need system-wide projections for capacity planning. However, most existing approaches forecast ED demand independently at a single level, ignoring the hierarchy linking hospitals, regions, and national systems. This can produce incoherent predictions, where hospital-level forecasts do not aggregate consistently to regional or national demand. We propose HierSTT, a hierarchical Transformer-based framework for coherent multi-level ED forecasting. HierSTT jointly predicts hospital, regional, and national level demand in a single end-to-end model. A Temporal Fusion Transformer captures national dynamics, while spatio-temporal Transformer encoder-decoder modules model regional and hospital demand conditioned on higher-level forecasts. A coherence-aware loss penalizes cross-level inconsistencies during training. We further introduce a nationwide Portuguese ED dataset covering 81 hospitals across 5 regional health administrations, with heterogeneous covariates at each level. Experiments show that HierSTT reduces average WAPE by 32\% relative to the best non-hierarchical deep learning baseline and outperforms all classical hierarchical reconciliation methods, while producing near-coherent predictions across levels. Additional resources associated with this work are available at https://github.com/FilipaLino/HierSTT.

</details>


### [79] [Voronoi Histograms for Adaptive Vectorization of Expected Persistence Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27126)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Voronoi图的直方图方法对预期持续图（EPD）进行向量化，无需显式光滑点变换模型，在特定条件下建立了稳定性界限并验证了其在分类和降维任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图（PD）能有效捕捉点云拓扑，但计算复杂度高；预期持续图（EPD）通过研究多个子集的拓扑来降低时间成本。现有EPD向量化方法依赖预定义的点变换（如高斯函数或景观函数），缺乏基于自适应划分的离散化方案。

Method: 提出基于Voronoi图直方图对EPD进行向量化，用自适应划分的计数替代光滑函数逼近；在分离性和归一化条件下建立稳定性界限，并刻画直方图表示保留Wasserstein尺度变化的条件。

Result: 在具有显著拓扑特征的真实数据集上，基于Voronoi直方图的EPD向量化在分类和降维任务中表现出有效性。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图是一种有效的EPD向量化方法，能够在无需显式光滑模型的情况下保持拓扑特征的稳定性，并适用于实际机器学习任务。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对holonomy-cover决策过程（一类结构化POMDP），构建了稳定商这一最小马尔可夫充分统计量，证明观测与稳定类配对构成精确有限马尔可夫状态，在特定条件下达到最小记忆符号数，并提出Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，实验验证了从原始状态到商状态的精确压缩及最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体需要维护一个递归可更新的历史统计量以恢复马尔可夫性质，但最小这样的统计量通常是未知的。本文旨在刻画holonomy-cover决策过程中这一最小马尔可夫充分统计量。

Method: 构建稳定商（stable quotient）——保持一步奖励和商后继的最粗观测级抽象；证明观测与稳定类配对构成精确有限马尔可夫状态；在可达性和成对决策分离条件下证明最小记忆符号数；提出包含稳定类表示、有序边传输更新、局部类坐标识别及有限MDP RL主干的Holonomy Memory强化学习框架。

Result: 实现了从原始状态到商状态的精确压缩；在三个决策时记忆状态下达到完美配对顺序精度，匹配商预言机性能并优于非预言机基线；可重置诊断下最近邻原型类推断具有指数衰减误差。

Conclusion: 稳定商是holonomy-cover决策过程的最小马尔可夫充分统计量，Holonomy Memory RL框架能有效恢复隐状态并迁移有限MDP保证，显著优于非预言机方法。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Skillful forecasting of offshore winds from satellite scatterometer constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27152)
*Francesco Pinto,Luca Lanzilao,Paco Lopez Dekker,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WindCastNet是首个基于卫星散射计观测的海上风速风向临近预报框架，利用部分卷积LSTM网络处理时空不规则观测数据，在北海评估中相比HARMONIE MEPS模型在1小时和2小时提前期分别降低RMSE 23%和7%，为短时海上风电预测开辟了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风电的分钟到小时级准确预测对电网运行日益重要，但现有数值天气预报在此短提前期下未优化，初始条件精度主导预报技能；卫星散射计虽常规同化至NWP但此前未被直接用于预报。

Method: WindCastNet采用部分卷积长短期记忆网络，利用欧、中、印散射计星座的微波雷达观测，编码空间观测掩码和观测间隔，通过连续时间表示实现任意提前期的预报。

Result: 北海评估中，1小时和2小时提前期相比HARMONIE MEPS模型RMSE分别降低23%和7%；前3小时优于持续性预报9-15%；强风和非均匀流动条件下技能下降。

Conclusion: 卫星散射计星座可为短时海上风电预报提供独立且有竞争力的来源，并为热带气旋临近预报等海洋天气应用开辟新机会。

Abstract: Accurate intraday forecasts of offshore wind are becoming increasingly important for power system operation and the integration of growing shares of offshore wind energy. Operational forecasts rely predominantly on numerical weather prediction (NWP), which is not optimized for lead times of minutes to hours, where initial-condition accuracy dominates forecast skill. Although satellite scatterometer observations are routinely assimilated into NWP, they have not previously been used directly for forecasting. Here we present WindCastNet, the first satellite-based nowcasting framework for offshore wind speed and direction, introducing a new paradigm for intraday forecasting that learns from spatiotemporally irregular satellite observations. WindCastNet predicts offshore wind fields from observations acquired by satellite scatterometer constellations. WindCastNet employs a partial convolutional long short-term memory network that exploits microwave radar observations from the European, Chinese, and Indian scatterometers despite their irregular spatial coverage, asynchronous sampling, and variable revisit times. Spatial observation masks and inter-observation intervals are encoded, while a continuous temporal representation enables forecasts at arbitrary lead times. Evaluated over the North Sea, WindCastNet reduces the root-mean-square error by 23% and 7% relative to the HARMONIE MEPS model at lead times of 1 and 2 h, respectively, and outperforms persistence by 9-15% during the first three forecast hours. Forecast skill decreases under strong-wind conditions and spatially non-uniform flow. These results demonstrate that satellite scatterometer constellations can provide an independent and competitive source of short-term offshore wind forecasts, opening new opportunities for renewable energy forecasting but also broader marine weather applications, including tropical cyclone nowcasting.

</details>


### [82] [When Do Learned Diffusion Proposals Help Constraint Solving? A Controlled Study on Continuous Algebraic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27169)
*Quang Bui,Sparsh Roy,Akash Gundimeda,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究连续代数约束系统的求解问题，提出MARC方法（将系统转化为因子图，使用图神经网络扩散去噪器生成赋值，通过精确计算机代数能量下降精化，并由符号检查器验证）。实验表明，学习型提案仅在随机多起点失败的高维场景中优于随机重启，而在低维耦合场景中优势消失。在8个真实世界系统中，经典多起点全部求解成功，无一处于学习有利区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续代数约束系统需要同时解决两个问题：找到满足约束的值，以及对不可解系统进行结构增强。经典求解器擅长前者，但对后者仅能通过枚举处理。本文旨在探究学习型提案（如图神经网络扩散去噪器）相对于随机多起点策略的实际优势，并明确学习方法的适用区域。

Method: 提出MARC框架：将代数约束系统转化为因子图，使用图神经网络扩散去噪器生成候选赋值，通过精确计算机代数能量下降进行精化，最后由符号检查器验证解。同时提出候选条件修复排序器（candidate-conditioned repair ranker），在K个增广中选择最优，减少穷举搜索开销。实验对比学习型提案与随机多起点（相同精化预算），推导随机多起点成功概率公式 P = 1 - (1 - q(n))^K。

Result: 候选条件修复排序器在平衡非线性菜单准确率上达到0.997 vs 0.236（p<10^-70），跨种子平均0.982±0.006。学习型提案仅在维度升高、随机搜索失效时才优于随机多起点；低维耦合场景下与随机重启持平。公式P=1-(1-q(n))^K以单一测量常数无参数拟合整条曲线（MAE=0.012）。在8个真实世界系统（机器人、定位、优化、代数）中，经典多起点全部求解成功，无一处于学习有利区域。

Conclusion: 学习型提案对代数约束系统求解的改进具有明确的适用边界：仅在高维随机搜索失效区域有效，在低维或耦合场景无显著优势。真实世界系统多处于学习非有利区域，经典多起点策略仍是最可靠的方法。研究结果映射了学习型提案改善求解器的条件与局限。

Abstract: Solving a continuous algebraic constraint system requires two decisions: which values satisfy the constraints, and which structural augmentation renders an unsolvable system solvable. Classical solvers answer the first well and the second only by enumeration. On that discrete decision, a candidate-conditioned repair ranker choosing among K augmentations reaches the exhaustive-search ceiling at a fraction of the calls, outperforming random (0.997 vs 0.236 balanced nonlinear menu accuracy; p < 10^-70; 0.982 +/- 0.006 across seeds) and beating a budget-matched per-candidate probe on accuracy and cost. MARC turns such a system into a factor graph, over which a graph-neural diffusion denoiser proposes assignments, descent on an exact computer-algebra energy polishes them, and an exact symbolic checker certifies solutions. Evaluations of diffusion-based proposals rarely include one control: random multi-start under the same refinement budget. Applied to our system, it sharply curtails what the learned proposal contributes on the value decision. Does it beat random multi-start at choosing satisfying assignments? Only narrowly, in a predictable regime. Across trapped low-dimensional families it ties with random restart, but dominates in high dimension, where random search fails. Once variables couple, the advantage is gone. Since all methods share one polish and one checker, best-of-K random multi-start succeeds with probability exactly 1 - (1 - q(n))^K, where q(n) is single-start reachability; one measured constant, with no free parameters, reproduces the entire curve (mean absolute error 0.012). The favorable regime is not specific to our synthetic families: across eight real-world systems in robotics, positioning, optimization, and algebra, classical multi-start solved all eight, none in the learning-favorable regime. We map the regimes in which learned proposals improve solvers.

</details>


### [83] [Inverse Learning of Latent Risk-Neutral Densities from Irregular Option Quotes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27188)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk,Aadi Dash,Nicholas A. Welsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了期权定价准确性与隐含风险中性密度恢复准确性之间的差异，通过合成基准和NIFTY市场数据基准进行对比分析。研究发现两成分对数正态混合模型在合成基准上表现最优，但不同模型各有优势（如DeepONet在分位数和方差误差上更优），且数值条件分析揭示了定价方向存在大量冗余。在真实市场数据上，测试时适应可提升DeepONet表现，但混合模型和SVI拟合仍更准确。结论支持任务依赖的归纳偏置，不存在通用最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准的期权价格并不等同于能够准确恢复潜在的隐含风险中性密度，这一区别在现有文献中未被充分研究。本文旨在通过两个互补基准（可控合成基准和实际市场基准）系统性地比较不同模型在这两个任务上的表现差异。

Method: 采用两个基准进行对比：1) 合成基准（已知真实密度，用于潜在评估）；2) NIFTY指数期权时间序列基准（仅测试保留的市场价格）。比较模型包括两成分对数正态混合模型、DeepONet、quote transformer、SVI等。通过L1误差、Wasserstein距离、固定尾部误差等指标评估，并进行数值条件分析。

Result: 1) 合成基准上两成分对数正态混合模型在聚合价格、L1、Wasserstein和固定尾部误差上最低；2) DeepONet将1%分位数和方差误差降低39.0%和34.6%；3) quote transformer在结构误设的Merton族上将L1降低16.4%；4) 数值条件分析显示95/126的定价方向是数值零空间，两个L1距离为0.061的密度产生相同价格；5) 测试时适应将DeepONet RMSE降低28.3%，但混合模型和SVI拟合更准确。

Conclusion: 不存在能够同时在价格预测和密度恢复两个任务上表现最优的通用模型，模型选择应基于目标任务（价格预测vs密度恢复）的归纳偏置。期权定价准确性不等于密度恢复准确性，数值条件分析解释了排名差异的原因。

Abstract: Accurate option prices do not imply accurate recovery of the latent risk-neutral density. We study this distinction with two complementary benchmarks. A controlled benchmark exposes simulator-truth densities for latent evaluation, while a chronological NIFTY benchmark tests only held-out market prices. A two-component lognormal mixture has the lowest aggregate price, $L^1$, Wasserstein, and fixed-tail errors on the synthetic benchmark. Learned operators retain narrower strengths: DeepONet reduces 1% quantile and variance error by 39.0% and 34.6% relative to the mixture, and a quote transformer reduces $L^1$ by 16.4% on the structurally misspecified Merton family. A numerical conditioning analysis explains why these rankings can differ: after enforcing mass and forward constraints, 95 of 126 pricing directions are numerically null, and two densities separated by $L^1 = 0.061$ produce identical prices on the covered strikes. On 524 held-out NIFTY calls, validation-selected test-time adaptation reduces DeepONet RMSE by 28.3%, but per-expiry mixture and SVI fits remain much more accurate. The evidence supports target-dependent inductive bias, not a universal winner.

</details>


### [84] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受果蝇感知机制启发的分类式回归方法，通过有限局部模式库替代全局代理模型，利用查询与存储模式的相似性进行加权重建预测，适用于非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回归方法依赖复杂的全局代理模型，计算和存储开销大，缺乏对精度、存储和推理成本之间权衡的显式控制。受果蝇感知机制的启发，探索用有限局部模式替代全局模型。

Method: 构建有限局部模式库；通过相似性度量将查询与存储模式匹配；对匹配模式的响应进行加权重建生成预测；采用离线-在线工作流，离线阶段从数据或控制方程中提取模式，在线阶段仅需相似度评估和响应聚合。

Result: 该方法降低了计算和内存需求，提供了对精度、存储和推理成本之间权衡的显式控制。

Conclusion: 分类式回归框架能有效处理非线性输入输出关系，在动力系统、数据回归和物理信息学习等任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [85] [Do You Really Need to Pretrain Q-Functions for Online RL Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27203)
*Perry Dong,Ron Polonsky,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Fin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究价值型强化学习中Q函数预训练对微调的影响，发现朴素Q函数预训练效果有限，原因是预训练阶段学习的Q函数与在线微调收敛的Q函数存在根本性不匹配。基于此发现，提出集成策略初始化方法（IPE），通过训练多个多样化策略并利用其混合轨迹来引导Q函数学习，在连续控制任务上取得1.26倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于价值的强化学习中，预训练-微调范式下Q函数是否需要预训练存在争议：传统观点认为应该预训练，但近期研究表明随机初始化Q函数的在线RL也能取得高性能。本文旨在系统研究Q函数预训练是否真的有助于在预训练基策略上进行微调。

Method: 1) 系统实验发现朴素Q函数预训练效果有限，归因于预训练Q函数与在线微调收敛Q函数之间的不匹配；2) 提出Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE)方法，训练多个多样化策略，利用其混合轨迹引导在线RL中的Q函数学习。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，IPE相比朴素Q函数预训练平均提升1.26倍微调性能。

Conclusion: 朴素Q函数预训练对微调帮助有限，IPE方法通过策略集成有效缓解了Q函数不匹配问题，显著提升微调性能。

Abstract: Pre-training followed by fine-tuning has become the dominant recipe for learning performant policies, and in value-based reinforcement learning (RL) this raises a natural question: given a pretrained policy, should the Q-function be pretrained on offline data too? Conventional wisdom suggests it should, but recent results show that online RL with a randomly-initialized Q-function can result in highly performant and reliable policies without needing to pretrain the Q-function. In this paper, we systematically study whether pretraining the Q-function actually helps when fine-tuning on top of a pretrained base policy. We find, surprisingly, that naive Q-function pretraining often provides little benefit over random initialization. We show this stems from a fundamental mismatch: the Q-function learned during pretraining targets the pretrained policy's Q-function, not the Q-function that online fine-tuning converges to, and this gap persists even after offline value maximization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE), a simple method that trains multiple diverse policies and uses their pooled rollouts to bootstrap the Q-function learning in online RL. Across a suite of challenging continuous control benchmarks, IPE yields an average 1.26x improvement in fine-tuning performance over naive Q-function pre-training.

</details>

### [86] [Route-Block Membership Selects Packed-AWQ Arithmetic: A Controlled Single-Fixture Mechanism Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26316)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过Qwen3-Coder AWG模型在特定硬件和软件环境下的实验，证明了MoE推理中路由-块预处理操作并非简单的"簿记"工作，而是决定了精确的矩阵乘法和算术轨迹。不同的预处理历史会产生不同的原生对齐和精确轨迹，通过干预路由排列和块边界可以转移完整的轨迹。研究确认了70个有效的冷过程，并排除了7个需要扰动的案例。该结果仅限于特定测试环境，不涉及通用性、分配器、可移植性或服务影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE推理中的路由对齐和块填充预处理通常被视为无关紧要的"簿记"操作，但本文试图通过严格的实验验证这些预处理步骤是否实际上决定了后续精确的算术计算轨迹，从而揭示其在推理过程中的因果机制作用。

Method: 在预指定的Qwen3-Coder AWG模型第6层、固定vLLM/Marlin构建和RTX 3090运行时环境下，通过路由-块干预实验比较不同预处理历史产生的原生对齐和精确轨迹。具体干预包括：注入相反对齐、交换块内两条路由、交换跨专家块边界的路由、强制单切片200块网格等操作，并观察轨迹变化。

Result: 不同的固定预处理历史产生不同的原生对齐和精确轨迹；注入相反对齐可转移W13、激活、routed-W2和最终输出；块内路由置换保留原生轨迹，而跨块边界路由置换可转移完整轨迹；源和二进制的调度几何将块映射为直接/全K和分割/全局归约两类；强制单切片200块网格使W13按位相等；稳定规范构建使两种历史收敛到第三个精确轨迹。确认70个有效冷过程，排除7个扰动案例。

Conclusion: MoE推理中的路由对齐和块填充预处理并非简单的簿记操作，而是对后续精确算术轨迹具有因果决定作用。但这一结论仅限于特定测试夹具，不能推广为通用性、分配器、可移植性或服务影响方面的论断。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) inference first aligns routed tokens into padded expert blocks, then executes packed quantized matrix multiplication over those blocks. This preprocessing is often treated as bookkeeping. In one pre-specified Qwen3-Coder AWQ layer-6 fixture on a pinned vLLM/Marlin build and RTX 3090 runtime, we show that the tested route-block interventions select exact packed arithmetic trajectories. Two fixed preconstruction histories produced distinct native alignments and exact trajectories. Injecting the opposite alignment transferred W13, activation, routed-W2, and final outputs. Permuting two routes within one block preserved each native trajectory, while exchanging two prior-data-selected routes across the boundary between expert-106 blocks 40 and 41 transferred the complete opposite trajectory. Source- and binary-derived schedule geometry maps those blocks to direct/full-K and split/global-reduction classes. Forcing a single-slice 200-block grid made W13 bitwise equal. Stable canonical construction made both histories converge to a third exact trajectory. The confirmatory cohort contains 70 valid cold processes and seven required perturbation rejections. This is a causal mechanism result for one fixture, not a prevalence, allocator, portability, or serving-impact claim.

</details>


### [87] [The Fabric Is the Cluster Driver: Cross-Layer eBPF Policies for GPU-CXL Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26335)
*Yiwei Yang,Andi Quinn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: fabric_ext是一个基于eBPF的中间件编译器和运行时，用于在GPU-CXL（Compute Express Link）互连架构上实现可扩展的操作系统策略。它通过“语义移动图”抽象，让单一策略程序能够在GPU、驱动/运行时、DPU/NIC、CXL交换机和近内存钩子上执行，编译器将该图降级为每设备eBPF程序，并在fabric边缘利用近Type-2小核心作为硬件JIT和状态管理器来执行数据驱动的数据流操作。典型用例是LLM预填充阶段中注意力流和前馈网络流在异构计算和存储设备间的协同执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的操作系统策略（如内存管理、数据移动、安全监控等）缺乏可扩展性，难以跨越GPU、DPU、CXL交换机等异构设备组成的fabric进行统一表达和执行。随着LLM等大规模AI工作负载的兴起，数据需要在GPU计算、DPU网络处理、CXL近内存计算等多个域之间高效流动，亟需一种可编程、安全且高效的跨设备策略执行框架。

Method: 提出fabric_ext系统，核心包括：(1) 语义移动图（Semantic Movement Graph）作为高级抽象，描述数据移动的字节数、步长、复用距离、读写比、源目、排序要求、别名集、所有权和变换操作（Move/Quantize/Compress等）；(2) 编译器将语义移动图降级为每设备eBPF程序、验证器义务、一致性分级的BPF映射以及bpftime和dputime的工件；(3) 运行时利用近Type-2小核心作为硬件JIT和状态管理器，将验证后的移动描述符特化为本地拷贝、放置、排序和变换命令，由冯·诺依曼岛（内存+DMA+计算引擎）执行数据流；(4) 在数据路径旁放置观察点，实时监控队列、DMA完成、内存放置和所有权转移。

Result: 以LLM预填充阶段为典型压力测试场景：注意力流需要传输KV块和规约结果，前馈网络流需要传输激活值、权重和可压缩中间结果，单一请求需要跨越GPU张量执行、DPU/NIC事件执行和CXL或交换机本地数据流岛。fabric_ext能够在这些异构执行域间实现统一的策略执行。

Conclusion: fabric_ext提出了一种基于eBPF的、横跨GPU-CXL fabric的扩展式OS策略执行框架，通过语义移动图抽象、编译器降级和近设备硬件JIT执行，实现了跨异构设备的安全、可编程数据移动策略，特别适用于LLM等大规模AI工作负载中的复杂数据流管理需求。

Abstract: We present fabric_ext, an eBPF middleware compiler and runtime for extensible OS policies over GPU--CXL fabrics. fabric_ext lets one policy program execute across GPU hooks, driver/runtime hooks, DPU/NIC hooks, and CXL switch or near-memory hooks. The key abstraction is a semantic movement graph: edges describe bytes, stride, reuse distance, read/write ratio, source and destination, ordering requirement, alias set, ownership, and transformations such as Move, Quantize, Compress, Checksum, Filter, Reduce, Scatter/Gather, Replicate, and Persist. The compiler lowers this graph into per-device eBPF programs, verifier obligations, consistency-classed BPF maps, and artifacts for bpftime and dputime. At the fabric edge, fabric_ext treats a near-Type-2 small core as a hardware-JIT and state manager: it specializes verified movement descriptors into local copy, placement, ordering, and transformation commands, while the surrounding Von Neumann island of memory, DMA, and compute engines performs the dataflow. Because this dataflow is data-driven, fabric_ext also places observation beside the island, where queues, DMA completions, memory placement, and ownership transitions are visible as they happen. The canonical stress case is LLM prefill: attention streams KV blocks and reductions while FFN streams activations, weights, and compressible intermediates, forcing one request to cross GPU tensor execution, DPU/NIC event execution, and CXL or switch-local dataflow islands.

</details>


### [88] [StrataCL: Fabric-Native Communication Library for Production Supernodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26444)
*Tiancheng Hu,Jin Qin,Yuzheng Wang,Ke Liu,TangShengsheng Li,Sheng Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Tianlun Hu,Wei Wang,Lijun Li,Jingbin Zhou,Xiaoming Bao,Hongwei Sun,Jieru Zhao,Huimin Cui,Tao Xie,Chenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StrataCL是一种面向生产级超级节点的零冗余、原生通信库，通过注册即分配策略实现用户缓冲区直接通信，并利用负载均衡的NPU核心分区和NPU驱动的SDMA卸载来优化通信效率。在华为CloudMatrix384上，集体通信带宽提升1.6倍，MoE调度/合并带宽提升1.4倍，LLM推理吞吐量提升1.9倍，P99 TTFT降低2.2倍，LLM和Recsys训练迭代时间分别减少1.4倍和1.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通信库采用缓冲区中心设计，用户缓冲区和通信缓冲区分离管理，导致冗余数据拷贝或昂贵的用户缓冲区注册开销，成为分布式AI工作负载的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出注册即分配（registration-on-allocation）策略实现用户缓冲区直接通信；设计负载均衡的NPU核心分区和NPU驱动的SDMA卸载通信算子，以充分利用超级节点架构特性。

Result: 在华为CloudMatrix384上，集体通信带宽提升1.6倍，MoE调度/合并带宽提升1.4倍；LLM推理吞吐量提升1.9倍，P99 TTFT降低2.2倍，LLM和Recsys训练迭代时间分别减少1.4倍和1.3倍。

Conclusion: StrataCL通过零冗余设计和原生硬件感知优化，显著提升了生产级超级节点上分布式AI工作负载的通信效率和端到端性能。

Abstract: Modern distributed AI workloads run across hundreds of accelerators, making communication a major bottleneck. Existing communication libraries remain largely buffer-centric because user and communication buffers are managed separately, causing redundant data copies or costly user-buffer registration. This paper presents StrataCL, a zero-redundancy and fabric-native communication library for production supernodes. StrataCL introduces registration-on-allocation to realize user-buffer direct communication, and designs communication operators with workload-balanced NPU-core partitioning and NPU-driven SDMA offloading to exploit supernode architecture features. On the Huawei CloudMatrix384, StrataCL improves collective bus bandwidth by up to 1.6x and improves MoE dispatch/combine bus bandwidth by up to 1.4x. Across three production workloads, StrataCL improves LLM inference throughput by 1.9x, reduces P99 TTFT by 2.2x, and reduces LLM and Recsys training iteration time by 1.4x and 1.3x, respectively.

</details>


### [89] [DualDecoder: Accelerate Long Context LLM Inference by Predictive Prefetch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26475)
*Zuning Liang,Zhiyi Yao,Qi Chen,Yuedong Xu,Hao Dai,Zhiqiang Ding,Tongkai Yang,Jinlong Hou,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DualDecoder是一个面向长上下文LLM推理的轻量级服务系统，通过利用推测令牌（speculated token）预测关键KV条目，实现从主机内存高效预取稀疏KV缓存，消除了传统方法中GPU内存辅助状态的开销，在保持解码延迟和模型质量的同时，将解码吞吐量提升高达2.62倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理面临严重的内存墙问题——KV缓存随上下文长度和请求并发度线性增长。现有稀疏KV缓存方法将大多数KV条目卸载到主机内存，但在GPU内存中引入了大量辅助状态用于KV检索管理。在高并发工作负载下，这些辅助状态成为新的瓶颈。

Method: 核心思路是下一个令牌解码所需的关键KV条目可以从先前推测的令牌中准确预测。基于此可预测性，KV检索可以主动预取并与解码计算重叠。具体包括：(1) 双令牌解码流水线，以可忽略的计算开销准确识别关键KV条目；(2) 层感知传输调度，将KV预取与模型计算重叠；(3) 层范围内存管理器，减少GPU运行时缓冲区。

Result: 实验结果表明，DualDecoder相较于最先进的系统，解码吞吐量提升高达2.62倍，同时保持了解码延迟和模型质量。

Conclusion: 通过推测令牌预测关键KV条目进行预取，可以有效消除GPU端辅助状态的内存开销，为长上下文LLM推理提供高效、轻量的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is becoming a fundamental capability for modern LLM serving, especially driven by emerging agentic applications. Yet it faces a severe memory wall that the KV cache scales proportionally with increasing context length and request concurrency. Existing sparse KV cache methods offload most KV entries to host memory and retrieve only the critical KV entries needed by each decoding step. However, they commonly introduce substantial auxiliary states in GPU memory for KV retrieval management. Our measurements show that these often-overlooked auxiliary states introduce significant memory overhead and become a new bottleneck under high-concurrency workloads.
  In this paper, we present DualDecoder, a lightweight serving system for long-context LLM inference that enables efficient sparse KV cache retrieval from host memory. Our key insight is that the critical KV entries required for decoding the next token can be accurately predicted from the preceding speculated token. This predictability enables KV retrieval to be proactively prefetched and overlapped with decoding computation, effectively eliminating the GPU memory overhead of auxiliary states. To achieve this prefetching efficiently, DualDecoder leverages a novel dual-token decoding pipeline that accurately identifies critical KV entries with negligible computational overhead, and designs a layer-aware transfer schedule to overlap KV prefetching with model computation and a layer-scoped memory manager to reduce the GPU runtime buffer. Experimental results show that DualDecoder improves decoding throughput by up to 2.62$\times$ over state-of-the-art systems while preserving decoding latency and model quality.

</details>


### [90] [Safety-Gated Autoscaling: A Multi-Layered Defense Architecture for Kubernetes Vertical Resource Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26503)
*Azra Karakaya,Erva Şengül,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Intelligent Cluster Optimizer，一个基于 Kubernetes 的开源智能集群优化器，通过五层安全流水线（含线性回归内存泄漏检测阻塞门）实现容器工作负载的合理规格调整，在保证安全的前提下实现 20-40% 的预估成本节约，泄漏检测准确率达 83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes 资源管理困难，工程师过度预留 CPU 和内存导致大量浪费；现有自动扩缩器（HPA/VPA）是反应式的，存在滞后和过度预留问题，且可能通过为泄露工作负载分配更多内存来掩盖软件缺陷；预测性扩缩器只关注预测精度或运行于专有基础设施内；异常检测仅用于告警而无法阻止有害操作。

Method: 提出 Intelligent Cluster Optimizer，核心是五层安全流水线：1) SLA 监控，2) 断路器，3) 基于线性回归与 R² 评分的内存泄漏检测阻塞门（检测到泄漏则拒绝建议），4) HPA/PDB 冲突检测，5) 策略引擎，并支持回滚和干运行模式。建议生成采用百分位数分析和 Holt-Winters 预测，通过多目标 Pareto 优化在容器级别平衡。1118 个自动化测试（80.3% 覆盖率）及 GKE 真实部署验证。

Result: 在 Google Kubernetes Engine 上的真实部署中，合理规格调整在假设推演中预估可节约 20-40% 的成本；内存泄漏检测门达到 83% 的检测准确率。

Conclusion: 以安全为首要考量的开源 Kubernetes 优化器，通过五层安全流水线（尤其是阻塞式泄漏检测）在实现显著成本节约的同时，避免了因自动扩缩掩盖软件缺陷的风险。

Abstract: Kubernetes is the standard platform for orchestrating containerized applications, yet resource management remains difficult. To stay safe, engineers over-provision CPU and memory, leaving reserved but unused capacity that is the main source of wasted cost. The built-in Horizontal and Vertical Pod Autoscalers are reactive: they act only after a threshold is crossed, which causes lag, over-provisioning, and can mask software defects by granting a leaking workload more memory. Predictive autoscalers focus on improving forecasting accuracy or run inside proprietary infrastructure, and anomaly detection is used only to alert, never to block a harmful action. The Intelligent Cluster Optimizer is an open-source Kubernetes operator that right-sizes container workloads with safety as a first-class concern. Its central contribution is a five-layer safety pipeline where a memory-leak detector, based on linear regression with R^2 scoring, acts as a blocking gate: if a leak is detected the recommendation is rejected, so the optimizer never hides a bug by enlarging a broken container. The pipeline combines SLA monitoring, a circuit breaker, HPA/PDB conflict detection, and a policy engine, with rollback and dry-run mode for human approval. Recommendations are produced by percentile analysis and Holt-Winters forecasting, balanced through multi-objective Pareto optimization at the per-container level. We validated the system with 1118 automated tests at 80.3% coverage and a live deployment on Google Kubernetes Engine, where right-sizing produced estimated cost savings of 20--40% in what-if projections and the leak gate reached 83% detection accuracy.

</details>


### [91] [ServerlessT2I: Efficient Text-to-Image Workflow Serving on a Serverless Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26566)
*Xiaoxiao Jiang,Suyi Li,Sheng Yao,Tianyu Feng,Lingyun Yang,Dapeng Nie,Haoran Yang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ServerlessT2I是一个面向文本到图像工作流的无服务器原生系统，通过将工作流分解为独立管理的模型函数，实现按模型扩缩、声明式工作流组合、透明GPU驻留通信和公平感知调度，在相同GPU预算下支撑高达2倍请求率，或节省高达3倍GPU资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平台将T2I工作流部署为不透明GPU函数，单体设计掩盖了工作流结构，导致扩缩开销大、用户需管理底层GPU协调、多租户集群中细粒度公平性受限。

Method: 将T2I工作流分解为松耦合的模型函数并独立管理调度；利用空闲GPU内存构建数据平面以减少模型加载和数据通信开销；引入公平调度器实现多租户服务。

Result: 在相同GPU预算下，支撑高达2倍请求率；在固定请求率下，节省高达3倍GPU资源同时满足SLO。

Conclusion: ServerlessT2I通过细粒度模型级管理显著提升了T2I工作流服务的资源效率和吞吐性能。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) workflows are increasingly deployed on serverless platforms because users often compose customized workflows and invoke them intermittently. Existing platforms typically deploy each workflow as an opaque GPU function, provisioning, placing, and scaling all constituent models in the workflow together. This monolithic design obscures workflow structure, inflates scaling overhead, forces users to manage low-level GPU coordination, and limits fine-grained fairness in multi-tenant clusters. In this paper, we present ServerlessT2I, a serverless-native system that decomposes a T2I workflow into loosely coupled model functions that can be independently managed and scheduled. By explicitly managing individual model execution, ServerlessT2I enables per-model scaling, declarative workflow composition, transparent GPU-resident communication, and fairness-aware scheduling. To make this decomposition efficient, ServerlessT2I harvests slack GPU memory left idle by compute-bound T2I inference to build a data plane that reduces model loading and data communication overheads. \sys{} further introduces a fair scheduler for multi-tenant serving. Using production traces, ServerlessT2I sustains up to 2$\times$ higher request rates than existing T2I workflow serving systems with the same GPU budget; for a fixed request rate, it saves up to 3$\times$ GPU resources while satisfying service level objectives (SLOs).

</details>


### [92] [Nix to the Rescue for a Reproducible HPC-AI Software Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26688)
*Wenke Du,Jean-Marc Gratien,Raphael Gayno,Bruno Raffin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告了在无root权限、有限网络的生产级HPC环境下，使用Nix构建混合HPC/AI软件栈的经验，通过Nix的一致包布局、完整环境隔离和flake组合，解决了依赖发现、系统库泄漏和跨项目组合问题，并生成了Apptainer容器用于集群部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级HPC环境下可重现性难以保证：无root访问、互联网受限、软件栈横跨C/C++/Fortran/Python/MPI/GPU等多种运行时。传统环境模块和Conda方案依赖手动干预定位依赖、系统库泄漏到构建中、且无法跨项目组合，容器虽有助于部署但本身不保证可重现性。

Method: 采用Nix包管理器构建混合HPC/AI软件栈，利用其一致包布局、完整环境隔离和flake组合特性，统一管理C/C++和Python依赖，并通过CMake presets拆分开发与生产环境，最终生成Apptainer镜像部署到生产集群。

Result: Nix解决了依赖发现、系统库泄漏和跨项目组合问题，统一了C/C++和Python的声明式管理，并能自动生成部署容器。但与Spack和Guix存在权衡，且Nixpkgs中ML包覆盖率存在缺口。

Conclusion: Nix在生产级HPC环境中是一种可行的混合HPC/AI软件栈可重现构建方案，但仍有改进空间，特别是在ML包覆盖方面。

Abstract: Reproducibility in HPC remains difficult under the constraints of production supercomputers: no root access, limited internet, and software stacks that increasingly span C/C++, Fortran, Python, MPI, and GPU runtimes. Traditional approaches based on environment modules and Conda require manual intervention to locate dependencies, leak system libraries into builds, and fail to compose across projects. Containers help with deployment but do not by themselves guarantee reproducibility. We report on our experience building a hybrid HPC/AI software stack with Nix, covering local development on a workstation without root and remote deployment as an Apptainer image on a production cluster. Nix's consistent package layout, full environment isolation, and flake-based composition resolve the dependency discovery, leakage, and composition problems we encountered, while unifying C/C++ and Python management under a single declarative specification that also generates the deployment container. We discuss trade-offs against Spack and Guix, the development-versus-production split addressed via CMake presets, and current gaps in ML package coverage in Nixpkgs.

</details>


### [93] [Mind the Gap: The Disconnect Between Synthetic and Natural Edge Weights in Parallel Single-Source Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26821)
*Marco D'Antonio,Thai Son Mai,Hans Vandierendonck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文发现当前并行单源最短路径（SSSP）算法的基准测试普遍使用均匀分布的合成边权重，但这与真实图数据的重尾权重分布严重不符。通过对17个真实图与6种合成分布的统计对比，以及对7种并行SSSP算法的全面评估，证明合成均匀权重不仅改变了最优参数配置，甚至颠倒了算法的性能排序，挑战了现有基准测试标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SSSP算法评估普遍使用合成均匀权重，而真实图权重分布呈现重尾特征，导致评估结果与真实性能脱节，有必要系统性地探究这种测试方法是否对算法性能产生误导性偏差。

Method: 1) 统计表征17个来自不同领域的真实图权重分布；2) 将其与文献中使用的6种合成分布进行对比；3) 对7种最先进并行SSSP算法进行全面评估，分析其对边权重的敏感性。

Result: 合成均匀权重会改变算法的最优参数配置，并且能够颠倒算法之间的性能排序，即对算法的性能评估产生了系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 现有基准测试标准存在偏差，研究呼吁采用更贴近真实权重分布的测试方法，为严谨的SSSP算法设计提供实践指导。

Abstract: Scientific research works often evaluate Parallel Single-Source Shortest Path (SSSP) algorithms using synthetic, uniformly distributed edge weights. However, real-world graphs exhibit very different, often heavy-tailed, weight distributions. This creates a disconnect between how algorithms are evaluated and their real-world performance, since most SSSP implementations inherently rely on the weight distribution for parameter tuning and work efficiency. In this paper, we explore whether current benchmarking methods unintentionally bias the performance results of these algorithms. To this end, we statistically characterize the weight distributions of 17 real-world graphs from a variety of domains and contrast them with six synthetic distributions used in the literature. Through a comprehensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art parallel SSSP algorithms, we demonstrate severe sensitivity to edge weights, and show that evaluating with synthetic uniform weights alters optimal parameter configurations and can invert the performance hierarchy. These findings challenge existing benchmarking standards and offer practical insights for rigorous SSSP algorithm design.

</details>


### [94] [Hybrid Workflow Composition for Extreme-Scale Data Processing: A Case Study on the HL-LHC (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26877)
*Alan Malta Rodrigues,Douglas Thain*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型仿真框架，用于研究HTC环境中工作流组合（任务集粒度）与系统约束（延迟、故障率、吞吐量、I/O带宽）之间的相互作用。通过探索高维参数空间，发现混合组合策略可实现最高3.8倍吞吐量提升和14.9倍网络开销降低，并提出了多指标目标函数以支持策略驱动的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTC环境中，工作流组合（即DAG中任务集的策略性分组）是影响执行开销和资源利用率的关键但常被忽视的挑战。

Method: 构建仿真框架，探索高维参数空间，量化不同工作流拓扑对任务粒度与系统约束（延迟、故障率、吞吐量、I/O带宽）的敏感性。

Result: 混合组合策略可带来3.8倍吞吐量提升和14.9倍网络开销降低；提出了多指标目标函数以导航吞吐量、I/O成本和CPU效率之间的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 为分布式系统中自动化工作流合成提供了理论基础，为下一代科学管道的可扩展建模奠定了基础。

Abstract: High-Throughput Computing (HTC) environments tailored for high-concurrency resource efficiency require sophisticated orchestration to manage petabyte-scale data across heterogeneous resources. A critical but often overlooked challenge is workflow composition: the strategic grouping of tasksets within a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) to mitigate execution overhead while maximizing resource utilization. This paper presents a novel simulation framework for characterizing the interplay between taskset granularity and system-level constraints (e.g., job latency, failure rate, throughput, and I/O bandwidth).
  By exploring a high-dimensional parameter space, we quantify the performance sensitivity of diverse workflow topologies. Our results demonstrate that hybrid composition strategies, which dynamically balance taskset independence with execution grouping, can yield up to 3.8x throughput increase and a 14.9x reduction in network overhead. We further propose a multi-metric objective function that enables policy-driven optimization, allowing system architects to navigate the Pareto frontier between throughput, I/O cost, and CPU efficiency. These findings provide a rigorous foundation for automated workflow synthesis in distributed systems, offering a scalable model for next-generation scientific pipelines. All artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [95] [InferScale: GPU-Native KV Injection for Personalized LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27090)
*Peter Li,Prashant Pandey*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: InferScale是一种GPU原生LLM记忆系统，通过预计算记忆事实的KV表示并直接注入vLLM的分页缓存，替代重复的prompt预填充，将TTFT与检索上下文大小解耦，在k=50时实现72-79%的TTFT降低和3.7-4.5倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统（如Mem0、MemGPT、Zep）在每次请求时重复预填充相同的记忆内容，导致TTFT随检索预算增长而增加，即使底层记忆被复用。亟需一种避免重复预填充、将记忆服务延迟与检索上下文大小解耦的方法。

Method: 1) 预计算每个记忆事实的KV表示并存储在GPU上；2) 引入Chunked RoPE，存储旋转前的键，注入时应用服务时的位置编码以支持动态组装的记忆；3) 提出Context-Window Encoding，将每个记忆事实与前面对话上下文的窗口一起编码，只缓存目标事实的KV，缓解独立编码缺失跨事实上下文的问题；4) 通过vLLM的KV-connector接口实现，无需修改引擎或微调模型。

Result: 在LoCoMo数据集上使用三个开源模型，k=50时TTFT降低72-79%（3.6-4.8倍），准确率60.3%（对比Mem0的63.3%，无需服务时重计算），并发负载下吞吐量提升3.7-4.5倍。

Conclusion: 可复用的KV状态能够将记忆条件下的服务延迟与检索到的上下文大小解耦，同时保持应用质量。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed with persistent personalized context, such as accumulated memory profiles or long conversation histories, that is shared across a user's many requests. Production memory systems (e.g., Mem0, MemGPT, and Zep) retrieve a relevant subset of this memory and inject it into the prompt, forcing the serving engine to repeatedly prefill the same content. As the retrieval budget grows, time-to-first-token (TTFT) increases even though the underlying memory is reused across requests.
  We present InferScale, a GPU-native LLM memory system that replaces repeated prompt prefilling with reusable KV state. InferScale precomputes each memory fact's KV representation, stores it alongside a semantic embedding on the GPU, retrieves relevant facts at serving time, and injects their KV directly into vLLM's paged cache. To support dynamically assembled memories under rotary position embeddings, we introduce Chunked RoPE, which stores keys before rotation and applies their serving-time positions during injection. However, encoding memory facts independently omits the cross-fact context available during joint prefilling. We mitigate this with Context-Window Encoding, which encodes each memory fact together with a small window of preceding conversation context while caching only the target fact's KV.
  InferScale is implemented through vLLM's KV-connector interface, requiring neither engine modifications nor model fine-tuning. Across three open-weight models on LoCoMo, InferScale keeps TTFT nearly constant as the retrieval budget increases: at k=50 it reduces TTFT by 72-79% (3.6-4.8x), achieves 60.3% accuracy versus 63.3% for Mem0 without serving-time recomputation, and delivers 3.7-4.5x the throughput under concurrent load. Reusable KV state thus decouples memory-conditioned serving latency from retrieved-context size while preserving application quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针和均值消融实验，揭示了强化学习训练的大规模推理模型相比监督微调模型，在数学推理任务中具有更线性可分的表示结构和层级化的关键层分布，从而解释了其性能优势；同时发现令牌分配差异更多取决于训练流程而非RL与SFT本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习训练的推理模型相比监督微调模型在数学推理任务中表现更优的神经表征机制基础。

Method: 1) 线性探针：基于层间隐藏状态训练分类器，预测答案正确性，比较RL与SFT模型的表征线性可分性；2) 均值消融研究：逐层消融神经元，评估各层对模型性能的关键程度；3) 重复采样分析：跨问题多次采样，分析令牌计数变异性以评估自适应计算分配。

Result: 1) RL模型在预测答案正确性上准确率更高，表征更线性可分且结构化；2) RL模型形成层级化架构，深层逐层变得更关键，而SFT模型重要性均匀分布；3) 部分RL模型令牌分配变异性高于SFT，部分则一致，表明令牌分配更多取决于训练管线而非RL与SFT本身。

Conclusion: RL训练从根本上重构了模型表示和处理推理问题的方式；令牌分配变异性揭示了策略的稳定性与不确定性，有助于识别模型是拥有稳定策略还是欠确定的、潜在不可辨识的解行为。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [97] [Even More Deception: Objective Misalignment in Mixed-Motive LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26120)
*Marylou Fauchard,Florian Carichon,Margarida Carvalho,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用社交推理游戏《狼人杀》设计框架，通过修改单一智能体的目标（保持角色不变），系统评估了LLM驱动的多智能体系统中目标不一致对集体决策的影响。研究发现，目标不一致会破坏对抗性环境中的集体表现，且这种影响在信息不对称和专业化角色下更为严重。被篡改的智能体会发展出目标导向的推理策略，但这些策略在公开的廉价谈话行为中几乎不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多智能体系统在混合动机环境中部署日益增多，这些环境存在信息不对称和战略欺骗，导致与集体目标的不一致成为核心问题。现有研究缺乏系统评估这种目标不一致的方法框架。

Method: 基于社交推理游戏《狼人杀》设计实验框架，修改单一智能体的目标（保持其角色身份不变），对四种模型家族和规模的LLM、四种玩家角色、三种目标公式化进行实验，通过智能体内部推理和公开廉价谈话行为的双重分析，辅以游戏结果分析。

Result: 目标不一致破坏了敌对环境中的集体结果，且信息不对称和专业化角色加剧了这种效应；被篡改的智能体发展出依赖目标的独特推理策略，但这些策略在公开行为中几乎不可见。

Conclusion: 即使微妙的目标不一致也会深刻影响集体决策，凸显了为LLM多智能体系统设计有效缓解策略的必要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs)-powered multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in mixed-motive environments, where agents operate under asymmetric information and strategic deception due to conflicting or hidden objectives. In these settings, misalignment with collective goals becomes a central concern. We propose a novel framework for evaluating objective misalignment using the social deduction game Werewolf, modifying the objective of a single agent while preserving its assigned role. Across LLMs from four different model families and sizes, four player roles, and three objective formulations, we introduce a dual analysis of the agents' internal reasoning and their public cheap-talk behavior (i.e costless, non-binding communication that does not directly affect the agents' utilities), complemented by an analysis of game outcomes. Our results show that objective misalignment undermines outcomes in inherently adversarial environments, an effect exacerbated by asymmetric information and specialized roles. While compromised agents consistently develop distinct objective-dependent reasoning strategies, these adaptations remain largely invisible in their public behavior. More broadly, our findings suggest that even subtle objective misalignment can profoundly affect collective decision-making, highlighting the need for effective mitigation strategies for LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [98] [ClinLens: Towards Long-Horizon Coding Agents for Longitudinal Multimodal Clinical Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26155)
*Yuan Zhu,Ethan B. Liu,Frank Nie,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLINLENS是一个包含200个可执行任务的临床数据科学基准，跨越MIMIC数据库的五个模态资源。最强的模型-框架配置仅达到56.3%的STRICTPASS通过率，而参考的编码智能体解决了126个任务中的83个，揭示了可运行提交与正确临床分析之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多将医学问答、结构化表格推理或通用科学知识库孤立开来，缺乏能够处理临床数据科学智能体所需的异质性纵向记录并将其转化为可审计分析的综合评估基准。

Method: 构建CLINLENS基准，包含200个可执行任务，覆盖MIMIC数据库的五种模态（结构化电子病历、临床笔记、心电图、胸片、超声心动图）。采用4×5分类法，交叉4种患者时间范围与5种分析能力。通过"程序优先反向合成"方法，为每个任务生成包含评估器私有参考工作流的半原始数据包，检查所需工件、队列和时序语义以及最终答案。

Result: 在126个任务的固定测试集上，最强的24种标准化模型-框架配置仅达到56.3%的scope-macro STRICTPASS通过率（但EXECSUCCESS为100%）。参考编码智能体解决了83/126个任务，而适配到GPT-4o-mini的五种生物医学系统最高仅达到2.9%的scope-macro STRICTPASS。

Conclusion: 可运行提交与正确临床分析之间存在实质性差距，现有系统在临床数据科学分析方面仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Clinical data-science agents must transform heterogeneous longitudinal records into auditable analyses, yet existing benchmarks largely isolate medical question answering, structured-table reasoning, or generic scientific repositories. We introduce CLINLENS, a benchmark of 200 executable tasks over five linked MIMIC resources spanning structured electronic health records, notes, electrocardiograms, chest radiographs, and echocardiograms. A 4 x 5 taxonomy crosses four patient-time scopes with five analysis capabilities. Program-first reverse synthesis pairs each bounded semi-raw package with an evaluator-private reference workflow and checks required artifacts, cohort and temporal semantics, and the final answer. On a fixed 126-task suite, the strongest of 24 standardized model-scaffold configurations achieves 56.3% scope-macro STRICTPASS despite 100% EXECSUCCESS. For reference, a separately configured coding agent solves 83 of 126 tasks, while five biomedical systems adapted to GPT-4o-mini reach at most 2.9% scope-macro STRICTPASS. These results expose a substantial gap between runnable submissions and correct clinical analyses.

</details>


### [99] [When benchmark inferences do not compose: Projectibility in AI evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26159)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了AI基准测试结果到实际应用的主张链中，即使每个环节单独成立，链式推理的整体也未必成立。提出了非组合原则：相邻推论的组合需要端点对齐、假设一致，并传递依赖性和不确定性。通过法律研究案例、重新分析和模拟，展示了如何诊断基准到实际应用论证中的断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试结果很少能一步得出关键主张，评估者需要将其推广、解释、外推、迁移，并结合人类审查和下游后果的假设。现有效度中心方法要求每个主张都有证据，但即便每个环节有可靠链接，也无法自动保证整个链的可靠性——因为前一研究的目标可能不是下一研究的来源，系统、人群、结果或条件可能在接口处发生变化，共享数据或模型血缘可能使看似独立的支持实际相互依赖。

Method: 借鉴Goodman的"竞争性扩展"问题，利用基于论证的效度框架构建测试架构。提出项目性（projectibility）审计方法，通过法律研究案例的定性分析、以及重新分析和模拟的定量方法，展示聚合稳定性如何抹消后续推论所需的区分。

Result: 证明了非组合原则：支持相邻推论的证据组合起来未必能有效支持整体链式主张。法律研究案例显示，基准测试证据和部署研究各自成立但可能彼此平行（互不支撑）。重新分析和模拟表明，聚合稳定性会掩盖后续推论所需的关键区分。

Conclusion: 提出了项目性审计方法，用于诊断基准到实际应用论证中的非正当连接问题，为AI评估链的可靠性提供了新的诊断工具。

Abstract: An AI benchmark result rarely reaches a consequential claim in one step. Evaluators generalize it to further cases, interpret it as evidence of capability, extrapolate it to new tasks, transport it to another system or site, and combine it with assumptions about human review and downstream consequences. Validity-centred approaches require evidence for each claim. This paper identifies a further epistemic problem: warranted links don't automatically make a warranted chain. The target of one study may not be the source of the next; system, population, outcome, or conditions may change at the interface; and shared data or model lineage may make apparently independent support dependent. Projectibility concerns whether a bounded extension from observed to unobserved cases is warranted. Goodman supplies the problem of rival extensions; argument-based validity supplies an architecture for testing them. The paper's distinctive claim is a non-composition principle: support for adjacent projections warrants their composition only when endpoints and assumptions align and dependence and uncertainty are carried through. A legal-research case shows how benchmark evidence and a deployment study can each be sound while remaining parallel. A reanalysis and simulation show why aggregate stability can erase distinctions a later projection requires. The resulting projectibility audit diagnoses unsupported joins in benchmark-to-use arguments.

</details>


### [100] [GuideSkill: Evolving Executable LLM Agent Skills for Guideline-Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26160)
*Lang Cao,Yuhao Shen,Tianyang Luo,Simo Du,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuideSkill是一种外部推理层，将临床指南的疾病诊断标准编译为可执行函数，通过Zero（从指南初始化）和Evo（用病例-诊断对进化）两种模式，显著提升了LLM的诊断准确性（宏平均准确率提升13.45-18.49%），且无需更新骨干模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统在处理临床实践指南时，通常只是检索指南文本或通过训练吸收知识，而非真正执行其规则逻辑，导致诊断准确性受限。作者希望设计一种模型无关的机制，将指南中的程序性知识转化为可执行形式，并结合临床病例模式来提升诊断能力。

Method: 提出GuideSkill框架：1) GuideSkill-Zero将疾病标准编译为返回有序诊断支持分数的可执行函数；2) GuideSkill-Evo利用病例-诊断对来精炼已有技能并补充缺失诊断；3) 推理时LLM先提出鉴别诊断，然后为每个匹配的技能提取所需特征，最后将模型排序与执行的技能分数融合。

Result: 在四个基准和四个骨干模型上：GuideSkill-Zero相比指南RAG平均提升13.45%宏平均准确率；GuideSkill-Evo相比直接推理相对提升18.49%，金牌技能覆盖率从56.5%提升到99.5%；在Qwen3.5-9B上超过最强参数更新基线11.16%且无需更新骨干模型；专家评估显示技能临床合理且可接受。

Conclusion: 可执行技能是一种模型无关的有效机制，能够将指南推导的程序与病例推导的诊断模式相结合，显著提升LLM的临床诊断性能。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode diagnostic criteria, but LLM systems typically retrieve guideline text or absorb it through training rather than execute its rules. We introduce GuideSkill, an external reasoning layer that compiles disease-specific criteria into executable functions returning ordinal diagnostic-support scores. GuideSkill-Zero is initialized from guidelines, while GuideSkill-Evo uses case--diagnosis pairs to refine covered skills and add missing diagnoses. At inference, an LLM proposes a differential diagnosis, grounds the features required by each matched skill, and fuses its ranking with the executed skill scores. Across four benchmarks and four backbones, GuideSkill-Zero improves macro-average accuracy over guideline RAG by 13.45% on average. GuideSkill-Evo achieves the highest macro-average for every backbone, improves over direct inference by 18.49% relatively, and increases gold-label skill coverage from 56.5% to 99.5%. On Qwen3.5-9B, it also exceeds the strongest parameter-update baseline by 11.16% without updating the backbone. Expert evaluation further indicates that GuideSkill produces clinically sound and broadly acceptable skills, suggesting that its initialized and evolved rules are reliable and practically meaningful. These results support executable skills as a model-agnostic mechanism for combining guideline-derived procedures with case-derived diagnostic patterns.

</details>


### [101] [GoGoTB: Agentic RTL Verification with Specification-Grounded Coverage Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26181)
*Xin Xin,Jincheng Lou,Junhui Li,Jinglin Yan,Panda Xiao,Di Wu,Haixiao Li,Weicong Lu,Weijian Fan,Xinyu Qu,Yuxiang Zhao,Min Yu,Zhixiong Di,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoGoTB是一个基于智能体的端到端验证闭合框架，通过执行控制层、知识系统和规格驱动的覆盖率体系，在8个RTL设计上无需人工干预实现了100%环境生成成功率和平均98.4%行覆盖率、97.2%分支覆盖率、97.0%翻转覆盖率及83.2%功能覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的验证方法通过独立单轮调用生成各组件，缺乏共享上下文，导致接口不匹配未被检测，且报告的覆盖率与规格要求脱节，无法实现端到端的验证闭合。

Method: 提出GoGoTB智能体框架，包含三个子系统：1）智能体执行控制层，在每个工具和阶段边界将确定性执行与LLM推理分离；2）可演化知识系统，按需分发方法论和设计特定专业知识；3）规格驱动的覆盖率框架，将每个覆盖率桶锚定到命名的规格行为，使每个残留缺口可诊断根因并有针对性地修复。

Result: 在8个RTL设计上无需人工干预，GoGoTB实现100%环境生成成功率，平均行覆盖率98.4%、分支覆盖率97.0%、翻转覆盖率97.0%、功能覆盖率83.2%。此前尚无工作能在相同基准上成功生成完整验证环境或达到有意义的覆盖率。

Conclusion: GoGoTB通过智能体化设计有效解决了LLM在IC功能验证中的上下文缺失和覆盖率脱节问题，实现了全自动的高质量验证闭合，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Functional verification dominates integrated circuit (IC) front-end engineering effort, and a single missed bug that escapes to silicon can trigger a costly respin. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate this process, yet existing LLM-based approaches generate each component through independent single-turn calls with no shared context, leaving interface mismatches undetected and reported coverage disconnected from specification requirements. To address these challenges, we present GoGoTB, an agentic framework that achieves end-to-end verification closure through three subsystems: an agentic execution control layer, an evolvable knowledge system, and specification-grounded coverage closure. The execution control layer separates deterministic enforcement from LLM reasoning at every tool and stage boundary. The knowledge system dispatches methodology and design-specific expertise on demand. The coverage framework anchors every bin to a named specification behavior so that each residual gap has a diagnosable root cause and a targeted remedy. Tested on 8 register transfer level (RTL) designs without any human intervention, GoGoTB achieves 100\% environment generation success and averages 98.4\% line, 97.2\% branch, 97.0\% toggle, and 83.2\% functional coverage. No prior work successfully generates a complete verification environment or achieves meaningful coverage on the same benchmarks.

</details>


### [102] [Position: Evaluation Scores Are Perishable Knowledge Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26191)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出现有语言模型评估中，通过平均化聚合多源信号会导致"评估信任膨胀"现象，并提出应以最弱链接（weakest-link）聚合替代均值聚合，同时为评估结果附加形式性、范围和有效期等元数据，以提升评估的可信度和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法通过平均化聚合自动指标、LLM评判、人工评估和基准测试等多源信号，导致评估置信度超过最弱信号的可信度，产生信任膨胀。作者旨在纠正这一现象，提出更保守、更诚实的评估方法论。

Method: 基于链式思维分析、可能性逻辑和代数理论等研究传统，建立了参数化的最弱链接聚合算子族；结合构建智能体AI评估工具集的具体经验，提出评估结果应附带形式层次、范围声明和过期日期等元数据。并在HELM公共排行榜上以54个前沿模型在10个场景下进行实证对比。

Result: 在HELM排行榜上，按均值评分和按最弱链接评分排名前五的模型完全不重合，表明均值聚合会显著改变模型排名，导致误导性结论。

Conclusion: 评估分数应被视为具有形式性、范围和有效期三种属性的认知主张；应采用最弱链接聚合而非均值聚合，并附加显式元数据以确保评估结果的透明性和可信度。

Abstract: Evaluation methodologies for language models increasingly combine multiple signals, from automated metrics and LLM-as-judge ratings to human assessments and benchmark suite results. When these signals are aggregated via averaging, evaluation confidence can then substantially exceed the reliability of the weakest signal: a phenomenon we call trust inflation in evaluation. We argue that evaluation scores should be treated as epistemic claims with three properties: formality (human evaluation provides stronger evidence than an automated metric), scope (a benchmark result applies to the tested distribution, not universally), and validity windows (benchmark results expire as contamination accumulates and distributions shift). Several converging research traditions (chain-of-thought analysis, possibilistic logic, and algebraic theory) establish weakest-link aggregation as the conservative endpoint of a parameterized operator family controlled by a single pessimism parameter. Drawing on those traditions, and on concrete lessons from building an evaluation harness for agentic AI, we propose that evaluation results carry explicit metadata (formality tier, scope declaration, and expiration date) to make their epistemic status transparent. We illustrate the cost of mean aggregation on the public HELM leaderboard: across 54 frontier models on ten scenarios, the top-five models ranked by mean score and by weakest-link are completely disjoint.

</details>


### [103] [TraceCoder: Explainable and Auditable Code Generation with Position-Key Snippet Versioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26307)
*Rwaida Alssadi,Muntaser Syed,Balaji Kasula,Lamine Deen,Majed Alotaibi,Mohammed Alghamdi,Tyler Ton,Ali Alqarni,Marius Silaghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceCoder 提出了一套包含关系型片段历史模式、浏览器可视化工具和竞争性位置键索引方案的三重机制，使 LLM 驱动的代码生成过程变得可审计、可追溯、可回放。在 30 个算法任务上的评估显示，平均 Chg% 达到 30%，30% 的代码片段携带可追溯的修复事件记录，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码生成模型产生黑盒输出，代码背后的推理逻辑不可见，修复过程的演化转瞬即逝，无法进行事后审计，缺乏信任与可问责性。

Method: 提出三重互补机制：(i) 关系型片段历史模式——记录每个修复事件的基准引用、轮次、失败文本和 LLM 解释；(ii) 浏览器可视化工具——以热力图和悬停注释方式展示历史；(iii) 竞争性分数位置键索引方案——使用树节点分隔符分配稳定且字典序有序的标识符。

Result: 在 30 个算法编程任务上评估，10 个任务耗尽了 6 轮修复预算。平均 Chg% 达到 30%，30% 的代码片段携带可追溯的修复事件行（对比基线 21%）。三个详细案例研究展示了系统如何解释每个基准失败对最终代码的影响。

Conclusion: TraceCoder 使自动化代码生成的内部"叙事"变得可审计和可回放，这对于生产环境中的信任和问责至关重要。

Abstract: Contemporary LLM-based coding agents produce code as black-box outputs: the rationale behind each line is hidden, the evolution of the code through benchmark-driven repair is ephemeral, and post-hoc auditing is impossible. We present a code generation concept that addresses these shortcomings through three complementary mechanisms: (i) a relational snippet-history schema that records, per repair event, the benchmark reference, round number, failure text, and LLM explanation, enabling full provenance queries; (ii) a browser-based visualisation tool that renders this history as heat-mapped, hover-annotated source code; and (iii) a competitive fractional position-key indexing scheme with tree-node delimiters that assigns stable, lexicographically-ordered identifiers to each code snippet, enabling fine-grained tracking without disrupting surrounding lines. We evaluate TraceCoder on 30 algorithmic programming tasks spanning string processing, mathematical computation, and data-structure manipulation, across two provider configurations. Of these, 10 exhaust the 6-iteration budget on tasks with subtle edge-case behaviour. Mean Chg% reaches 30%, three in ten code snippets carry a traceable repair-event row, compared to 21% when using Gemini 2.0 Flash as sole provider on a 20-task subset. Three detailed case studies demonstrate how the system explains which specific benchmark failures shaped each line of the final program. The proposed mechanism makes the internal "narrative" of automated code generation auditable and replayable, a property essential for trust and accountability in production deployments.

</details>


### [104] [Exploring Structures in Physics Problems: Can AI Agents Discover Statistical Mechanical Mappings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26367)
*Wanyu Zhao,Wanbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StatMechBench-v0基准，用于评估LLM智能体能否从原始配分函数中发现统计力学映射（如转移到易处理表示）。实验表明，数值反馈能帮助智能体修复代码并恢复正确的配分函数，但智能体可能在通过数值检验的同时错误识别潜在可解类或低估计算复杂度，揭示了当前LLM推理的局限性，并呼吁超越数值一致的验证栈（如符号检查与结构不变量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论研究中的一个重要技能是将新问题转化为已知模型。作者希望探究基于LLM的AI智能体能否自主完成这种统计力学映射——从原始配分函数发现可解表示，从而评估AI在理论物理结构发现方面的能力。

Method: 构建了StatMechBench-v0基准，包含六类Ising型问题（涵盖转移矩阵方法、规范可移除无序、平面/Pfaffian结构）。采用"提出-验证-修正"（propose-verify-revise）智能体框架，在多种LLM和问题表述下进行系统评估。

Result: 数值反馈通常能帮助智能体修复代码并恢复正确配分函数；但智能体可能通过数值检验却误判潜在可解类别或低估计算复杂度，暴露出数值验证的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在理论物理的结构发现任务中存在推理局限性，未来需要构建超越数值一致性验证的完整验证栈，包含符号检查与结构不变量等方法。

Abstract: An important skill in theoretical physics is to recognize when a new problem can be transformed into a known model. We study this skill as an AI-agent task: can LLM-based agents discover statistical mechanical mappings from a raw partition function to a tractable representation? To probe this question, we introduce StatMechBench-v0, a benchmark of six Ising-type problems covering transfer-matrix methods, gauge-removable disorder, and planar/Pfaffian structure. We evaluate a simple propose-verify-revise agent across multiple LLMs and problem phrasings. The results show that numerical feedback often helps agents repair code and recover correct partition functions. However, agents can also pass the numerical checks while misidentifying the underlying tractable class or understating computational complexity. This both reveals limitations in current LLM reasoning and calls for a verification stack that goes beyond numerical agreement, incorporating, for example, symbolic checks and structural invariants. Our study provides an early evaluation and design directions for AI agents aimed at structural discovery in theoretical physics.

</details>


### [105] [CaM-Wolf: Causal-Aware Multimodal Agents for Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26393)
*Zheng Zhang,Nanjie Yao,Jiarui He,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaM-Wolf是首个集成多模态感知与生成的社会推理游戏AI代理，通过视频输入、因果推理强化学习训练和动画化身，在狼人杀游戏中实现更优的游戏表现和人机交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会推理游戏AI代理主要基于文本，忽略了人类社交互动中关键的多模态特征（如面部表情、肢体语言等），缺乏对多模态信息的感知与生成能力。

Method: CaM-Wolf包含三个关键组件：1）多模态感知模块，处理其他玩家的视频输入；2）因果感知推理器（Causal-aware Reasoner），通过强化学习训练建立可观察行为与隐藏角色之间的逻辑链条；3）动画化身呈现，实现多模态生成。

Result: 实验和用户研究表明，CaM-Wolf在代理游戏性能上优于现有方法，并显著提升了人机交互质量。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着向能够参与细微社会动态的、更类人AI代理迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) such as Werewolf have become challenging testbeds for AI agents. These games require complex social skills such as reasoning, deception, and collaboration. While recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in SDG agents, current approaches are predominantly text-based, overlooking the multimodal nature that is fundamental to human social interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce CaM-Wolf, the first SDG agent that integrates multimodal perception and generation. CaM-Wolf processes video inputs from other players, employs a causal-aware Reasoner trained via reinforcement learning to establish logical chains between observable behaviors and hidden roles, and presents itself through an animated avatar. Our experiments and user study show that CaM-Wolf achieves superior agent gameplay performance and enhances the quality of human-AI interaction. This work represents a significant advancement towards creating more human-like AI agents capable of participating in nuanced social dynamics. Our code is available at https://3dagentworld.github.io/avatar_wolf.

</details>
